\mbox{\hyperlink{soc_8h_ace56b149cfea256a704f92c73a3cb1a9}{snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+kcontrol\+\_\+component()}} -\/ Returns the component that registered the control @kcontrol\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} control for which to get the component

With snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+add\+\_\+codec\+\_\+controls() or via table based setup for either a C\+O\+D\+EC or component driver.
